$ cd /app/langgraph/libs/langgraph/tests && cat >> test_interruption.py <<'EOF'


class _Answer(BaseModel):
    age: int


def _single_interrupt_graph(
    checkpointer: BaseCheckpointSaver, response_schema: Any, accepted: list[Any]
) -> Any:
    class State(TypedDict):
        answer: Any

    def ask(_state: State) -> dict[str, Any]:
        answer = interrupt("age?", response_schema=response_schema)
        accepted.append(answer)
        return {"answer": answer}

    builder = StateGraph(State)
    builder.add_node("ask", ask)
    builder.add_edge(START, "ask")
    return builder.compile(checkpointer=checkpointer)


def test_interrupt_surfaces_response_schema(
    sync_checkpointer: BaseCheckpointSaver,
) -> None:
    dict_schema = {"type": "string", "minLength": 2}
    for thread_id, schema, expected in [
        ("dict", dict_schema, dict_schema),
        ("type", _Answer, TypeAdapter(_Answer).json_schema()),
        ("none", None, None),
    ]:
        graph = _single_interrupt_graph(sync_checkpointer, schema, [])
        config = {"configurable": {"thread_id": thread_id}}

        surfaced = graph.invoke({"answer": None}, config)["__interrupt__"][0]
        assert surfaced.response_schema == expected
        assert surfaced == Interrupt(
            value="age?", id=surfaced.id, response_schema=expected
        )
        assert graph.get_state(config).interrupts == (surfaced,)
        assert graph.get_state(config).tasks[0].interrupts == (surfaced,)
        debug = [
            chunk["payload"]
            for chunk in graph.stream(None, config, stream_mode="debug")
            if chunk["type"] == "task_result"
        ]
        rendered = {"value": "age?", "id": surfaced.id}
        if expected is not None:
            rendered["response_schema"] = expected
        assert debug[0]["interrupts"] == [rendered]

    assert repr(Interrupt(value="age?", id="1")) == "Interrupt(value='age?', 
id='1')"
    assert repr(Interrupt(value="age?", id="1", response_schema={"type": 
"integer"})) == (
        "Interrupt(value='age?', id='1', response_schema={'type': 'integer'})"
    )


def test_interrupt_dict_response_schema_is_not_enforced(
    sync_checkpointer: BaseCheckpointSaver,
) -> None:
    accepted: list[Any] = []
    graph = _single_interrupt_graph(sync_checkpointer, {"type": "integer"}, 
accepted)
    config = {"configurable": {"thread_id": "1"}}
    interrupt_id = graph.invoke({"answer": None}, config)["__interrupt__"][0].id

    assert graph.invoke(Command(resume={interrupt_id: "not an int"}), config) ==
{
        "answer": "not an int"
    }
    assert accepted == ["not an int"]


def test_interrupt_rejects_invalid_answer_without_keeping_it(
    sync_checkpointer: BaseCheckpointSaver, durability: Durability
) -> None:
    accepted: list[Any] = []
    graph = _single_interrupt_graph(sync_checkpointer, _Answer, accepted)
    config = {"configurable": {"thread_id": "1"}}
    pending = graph.invoke({"answer": None}, config, durability=durability)[
        "__interrupt__"
    ][0]

    for invalid in ({"age": "old"}, {}, Command(resume="old")):
        resume = invalid if isinstance(invalid, Command) else Command(
            resume={pending.id: invalid}
        )
        with pytest.raises(ValidationError) as exc_info:
            graph.invoke(resume, config, durability=durability)
        assert exc_info.value.error_count() == 1
        assert exc_info.value.errors()[0]["loc"] == ("age",)
        assert graph.get_state(config).interrupts == (pending,)
        assert accepted == []

    result = graph.invoke(
        Command(resume={pending.id: {"age": "41"}}), config, 
durability=durability
    )
    assert result == {"answer": _Answer(age=41)}
    assert accepted == [_Answer(age=41)]
    assert graph.get_state(config).interrupts == ()


async def test_interrupt_rejects_invalid_answer_without_keeping_it_async(
    async_checkpointer: BaseCheckpointSaver, durability: Durability
) -> None:
    accepted: list[Any] = []
    graph = _single_interrupt_graph(async_checkpointer, int, accepted)
    config = {"configurable": {"thread_id": "1"}}
    pending = (await graph.ainvoke({"answer": None}, config, 
durability=durability))[
        "__interrupt__"
    ][0]

    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        await graph.ainvoke(
            Command(resume={pending.id: "old"}), config, durability=durability
        )
    assert (await graph.aget_state(config)).interrupts == (pending,)

    result = await graph.ainvoke(
        Command(resume={pending.id: "41"}), config, durability=durability
    )
    assert result == {"answer": 41}
    assert accepted == [41]


def test_interrupt_keeps_earlier_answers_when_a_later_one_is_rejected(
    sync_checkpointer: BaseCheckpointSaver,
) -> None:
    class State(TypedDict):
        answers: list[Any]

    def ask(_state: State) -> dict[str, Any]:
        name = interrupt("name?", response_schema=str)
        age = interrupt("age?", response_schema=int)
        return {"answers": [name, age]}

    builder = StateGraph(State)
    builder.add_node("ask", ask)
    builder.add_edge(START, "ask")
    graph = builder.compile(checkpointer=sync_checkpointer)
    config = {"configurable": {"thread_id": "1"}}

    interrupt_id = graph.invoke({"answers": []}, config)["__interrupt__"][0].id
    second = graph.invoke(Command(resume={interrupt_id: "Ada"}), config)
    assert second["__interrupt__"][0].value == "age?"

    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        graph.invoke(Command(resume={interrupt_id: "old"}), config)
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        graph.invoke(Command(resume={interrupt_id: "older"}), config)

    assert graph.invoke(Command(resume={interrupt_id: "36"}), config) == {
        "answers": ["Ada", 36]
    }


def _parallel_graph(checkpointer: BaseCheckpointSaver, ran: list[str]) -> Any:
    class State(TypedDict):
        done: Annotated[list[str], operator.add]

    async def slow(_state: State) -> dict[str, Any]:
        interrupt("slow?", response_schema=bool)
        ran.append("slow-start")
        await asyncio.sleep(0.2)
        ran.append("slow-end")
        return {"done": ["slow"]}

    def fast(_state: State) -> dict[str, Any]:
        interrupt("fast?", response_schema=bool)
        ran.append("fast")
        return {"done": ["fast"]}

    builder = StateGraph(State)
    builder.add_node("slow", slow)
    builder.add_node("fast", fast)
    builder.add_edge(START, "slow")
    builder.add_edge(START, "fast")
    return builder.compile(checkpointer=checkpointer)


async def test_rejected_answer_lets_parallel_tasks_finish_once(
    async_checkpointer: BaseCheckpointSaver,
) -> None:
    ran: list[str] = []
    graph = _parallel_graph(async_checkpointer, ran)
    config = {"configurable": {"thread_id": "1"}}
    pending = {
        i.value: i.id
        for i in (await graph.ainvoke({"done": []}, config))["__interrupt__"]
    }

    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        await graph.ainvoke(
            Command(resume={pending["slow?"]: True, pending["fast?"]: "maybe"}),
            config,
        )
    assert ran == ["slow-start", "slow-end"]
    assert [i.value for i in (await graph.aget_state(config)).interrupts] == [
        "fast?"
    ]

    result = await graph.ainvoke(Command(resume={pending["fast?"]: True}), 
config)
    assert sorted(result["done"]) == ["fast", "slow"]
    assert ran == ["slow-start", "slow-end", "fast"]


def test_rejected_answer_in_subgraph(sync_checkpointer: BaseCheckpointSaver) -> 
None:
    class State(TypedDict):
        answer: Any

    def ask(_state: State) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {"answer": interrupt("age?", response_schema=int)}

    inner = StateGraph(State)
    inner.add_node("ask", ask)
    inner.add_edge(START, "ask")
    outer = StateGraph(State)
    outer.add_node("inner", inner.compile())
    outer.add_edge(START, "inner")
    graph = outer.compile(checkpointer=sync_checkpointer)
    config = {"configurable": {"thread_id": "1"}}
    pending = graph.invoke({"answer": None}, config)["__interrupt__"][0]

    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        graph.invoke(Command(resume={pending.id: "old"}), config)
    assert graph.get_state(config).interrupts == (pending,)

    assert graph.invoke(Command(resume={pending.id: "41"}), config) == 
{"answer": 41}

I'm checking what happens to the null resume write if the plain 
`Command(resume={"age": "old"})` call raises. It stays in the pending writes, so
the next map-based resume with `{"age": "41"}` gets accepted at index 0 and the 
null write is still sitting there after the superstep completes.

I'm weighing whether the null resume write left behind by a rejected plain 
`Command(resume=...)` causes trouble: it's already persisted in the 
checkpointer, so `invoke(None)` afterward would replay the rejected answer. I 
don't see an easy fix, since `put_writes` can't delete entries, so I'm leaning 
toward leaving it and documenting the limitation.

import asyncio

pytestmark = pytest.mark.anyio


    assert (await graph.aget_state(thread)).next == ()
    n_checkpoints = len([c async for c in graph.aget_state_history(thread)])
    assert n_checkpoints == (5 if durability != "exit" else 3)


class _Answer(BaseModel):
    age: int


def _single_interrupt_graph(
    checkpointer: BaseCheckpointSaver, response_schema: Any, accepted: list[Any]
) -> Any:
    class State(TypedDict):
        answer: Any

    def ask(_state: State) -> dict[str, Any]:
        answer = interrupt("age?", response_schema=response_schema)
        accepted.append(answer)
        return {"answer": answer}

    builder = StateGraph(State)
    builder.add_node("ask", ask)
    builder.add_edge(START, "ask")
    return builder.compile(checkpointer=checkpointer)


def test_interrupt_surfaces_response_schema(
    sync_checkpointer: BaseCheckpointSaver,
) -> None:
    dict_schema = {"type": "string", "minLength": 2}
    for thread_id, schema, expected in [
        ("dict", dict_schema, dict_schema),
        ("type", _Answer, TypeAdapter(_Answer).json_schema()),
        ("none", None, None),
    ]:
        graph = _single_interrupt_graph(sync_checkpointer, schema, [])
        config = {"configurable": {"thread_id": thread_id}}

        surfaced = graph.invoke({"answer": None}, config)["__interrupt__"][0]
        assert surfaced.response_schema == expected
        assert surfaced == Interrupt(

# --- per_call mode 
---------------------------------------------------------------


@tool
def search(query: str, limit: int = 5) -> str:
    """Search the web."""
    _RAN.append(("search", {"query": query, "limit": limit}))
    return f"results for {query}"


@tool
def delete_file(path: str, runtime: ToolRuntime) -> str:
    """Delete a file."""
    _RAN.append(("delete_file", {"path": path, "call_id": 
runtime.tool_call_id}))
    return f"deleted {path}"


_RAN: list[tuple[str, dict[str, Any]]] = []


@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
def _reset_ran() -> None:
    _RAN.clear()


def _per_call_agent(
    tool_calls: list[ToolCall],
    interrupt_on: dict[str, Any],
    *,
    tools: list[Any] | None = None,
    middleware: list[Any] | None = None,
    interrupt_mode: str = "per_call",
) -> Any:
    return create_agent(
        model=FakeToolCallingModel(tool_calls=[tool_calls, []]),
        tools=tools or [search, delete_file],
        middleware=[
            HumanInTheLoopMiddleware(interrupt_on, 
interrupt_mode=interrupt_mode),  # type: ignore[arg-type]
            *(middleware or []),
        ],
        checkpointer=InMemorySaver(),
    )


def _pauses(result: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Interrupt]:
    return {i.value["tool_call_id"]: i for i in result["__interrupt__"]}


def _tool_messages(result: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, ToolMessage]:
    return {m.tool_call_id: m for m in result["messages"] if isinstance(m, 
ToolMessage)}


def test_interrupt_mode_defaults_to_batched_and_rejects_unknown_modes() -> None:
    assert get_args(InterruptMode) == ("batched", "per_call")
    assert HumanInTheLoopMiddleware({"search": True}).interrupt_mode == 
"batched"
    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match=re.escape("`interrupt_mode` must be 'batched' or 'per_call', got 
'sometimes'."),
    ):
        HumanInTheLoopMiddleware({"search": True}, interrupt_mode="sometimes")  
# type: ignore[arg-type]


def test_per_call_pauses_each_listed_call_before_it_runs() -> None:
    runtimes: list[Any] = []

    def describe(tool_call: ToolCall, state: AgentState[Any], runtime: 
Runtime[Any]) -> str:
        runtimes.append(runtime)
        return f"Delete {tool_call['args']['path']}? ({len(state['messages'])} 
messages)"

    agent = _per_call_agent(
        [
            ToolCall(name="search", args={"query": "cats"}, id="1"),
            ToolCall(name="delete_file", args={"path": "/a"}, id="2"),
            ToolCall(name="search", args={"query": "skip"}, id="3"),
            ToolCall(name="delete_file", args={"path": "/b"}, id="4"),
        ],
        {
            "search": {
                "allowed_decisions": ["approve", "reject"],
                "description": "Search?",
                "when": lambda request: request.tool_call["args"]["query"] != 
"skip",
            },
            "delete_file": {"allowed_decisions": ["approve"], "description": 
describe},
        },
    )
    config: RunnableConfig = {"configurable": {"thread_id": "pauses"}}
    result = agent.invoke({"messages": [HumanMessage("go")]}, config)

    assert _RAN == [("search", {"query": "skip", "limit": 5})]
    pauses = _pauses(result)
    assert [pause.value for pause in pauses.values()] == [
        {
            "type": "tool_approval",
            "tool_call_id": "1",
            "name": "search",
            "args": {"query": "cats"},
            "description": "Search?",
        },
        {
            "type": "tool_approval",
            "tool_call_id": "2",
            "name": "delete_file",
            "args": {"path": "/a"},
            "description": "Delete /a? (2 messages)",
        },
        {
            "type": "tool_approval",
            "tool_call_id": "4",
            "name": "delete_file",
            "args": {"path": "/b"},
            "description": "Delete /b? (2 messages)",
        },
    ]
    assert all(type(runtime) is Runtime for runtime in runtimes)
    assert pauses["1"].response_schema == TypeAdapter(
        _decision_schema(["approve", "reject"], "search", search)
    ).json_schema()

    result = agent.invoke(
        Command(resume={pause.id: {"type": "approve"} for pause in 
pauses.values()}), config
    )
    assert "__interrupt__" not in result
    assert sorted(_tool_messages(result)) == ["1", "2", "3", "4"]
    assert [name for name, _ in _RAN].count("search") == 2
    assert [name for name, _ in _RAN].count("delete_file") == 2


def test_per_call_unlisted_tools_run_without_pausing() -> None:
    agent = _per_call_agent(
        [ToolCall(name="search", args={"query": "cats"}, id="1")], 
{"delete_file": True}
    )
    result = agent.invoke(
        {"messages": [HumanMessage("go")]}, {"configurable": {"thread_id": 
"unlisted"}}
    )
    assert "__interrupt__" not in result
    assert _RAN == [("search", {"query": "cats", "limit": 5})]


def test_decision_schema_structure() -> None:
    schema = TypeAdapter(
        _decision_schema(["reject", "edit", "approve", "respond"], "search", 
search)
    ).json_schema()

    assert schema["oneOf"] == [
        {"$ref": "#/$defs/RejectDecision"},
        {"$ref": "#/$defs/EditDecision"},
        {"$ref": "#/$defs/ApproveDecision"},
        {"$ref": "#/$defs/RespondDecision"},
    ]
    defs = schema["$defs"]
    assert defs["EditDecision"]["properties"]["edited_action"] == {
        "$ref": "#/$defs/EditedAction"
    }
    edited_action = defs["EditedAction"]["properties"]
    assert edited_action["name"]["const"] == "search"
    assert edited_action["args"] == {"$ref": "#/$defs/search"}
    assert defs["search"]["description"] == "Search the web."
    assert defs["search"]["properties"] == 
search.tool_call_schema.model_json_schema()[
        "properties"
    ]
    assert defs["search"]["required"] == ["query"]

    single = TypeAdapter(_decision_schema(["approve"], "search", 
search)).json_schema()
    assert single["properties"] == {"type": {"const": "approve", "title": 
"Type", "type": "string"}}
    assert "oneOf" not in single

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="deny"):
        _decision_schema(["approve", "deny"], "search")  # type: 
ignore[list-item]


def test_decision_schema_shows_but_does_not_enforce_unvalidated_args() -> None:
    json_schema_tool = StructuredTool(
        name="lookup",
        description="Look something up.",
        args_schema={
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {"key": {"type": "string"}},
            "required": ["key"],
        },
        func=lambda **kwargs: kwargs,
    )
    for tool_name, tool_ in [("lookup", json_schema_tool), ("ghost", None)]:
        adapter = TypeAdapter(_decision_schema(["edit"], tool_name, tool_))
        args_def = adapter.json_schema()["$defs"][tool_name]
        edit = {"type": "edit", "edited_action": {"name": tool_name, "args": 
{"other": 1}}}
        assert adapter.validate_python(edit) == edit
        with pytest.raises(ValidationError) as exc_info:
            adapter.validate_python({**edit, "edited_action": {"name": 
tool_name, "args": 1}})
        assert [e["loc"] for e in exc_info.value.errors()] == [("edited_action",
"args")]
        if tool_ is None:
            assert args_def == {"title": "ghost", "type": "object"}
        else:
            assert args_def["properties"] == {"key": {"type": "string"}}
            assert args_def["required"] == ["key"]
            assert args_def["description"] == "Look something up."


def test_per_call_decisions_produce_batched_tool_messages() -> None:
    tool_calls = [
        ToolCall(name="search", args={"query": "cats"}, id="1"),
        ToolCall(name="search", args={"query": "dogs"}, id="2"),
        ToolCall(name="search", args={"query": "owls"}, id="3"),
        ToolCall(name="search", args={"query": "bats"}, id="4"),
    ]
    decisions: list[dict[str, Any]] = [
        {"type": "approve"},
        {"type": "reject"},
        {"type": "reject", "message": "not now"},
        {"type": "respond", "message": "the human's answer"},
    ]
    batched = _per_call_agent(tool_calls, {"search": True}, 
interrupt_mode="batched")
    batched_config: RunnableConfig = {"configurable": {"thread_id": "batched"}}
    batched.invoke({"messages": [HumanMessage("go")]}, batched_config)
    expected = _tool_messages(
        batched.invoke(Command(resume={"decisions": decisions}), batched_config)
    )
    _RAN.clear()

    agent = _per_call_agent(tool_calls, {"search": True})
    config: RunnableConfig = {"configurable": {"thread_id": "per-call"}}
    pauses = _pauses(agent.invoke({"messages": [HumanMessage("go")]}, config))
    result = agent.invoke(
        Command(
            resume={pauses[call["id"]].id: d for call, d in zip(tool_calls, 
decisions)}  # type: ignore[index]
        ),
        config,
    )

    assert _RAN == [("search", {"query": "cats", "limit": 5})]
    messages = _tool_messages(result)
    for tool_call_id, message in expected.items():
        assert (messages[tool_call_id].content, messages[tool_call_id].status) 
== (
            message.content,
            message.status,
        )


def test_per_call_edit_runs_reviewers_args() -> None:
    agent = _per_call_agent(
        [
            ToolCall(name="search", args={"query": "cats"}, id="1"),
            ToolCall(name="delete_file", args={"path": "/a"}, id="2"),
        ],
        {"search": True, "delete_file": True},
    )
    config: RunnableConfig = {"configurable": {"thread_id": "edit"}}
    pauses = _pauses(agent.invoke({"messages": [HumanMessage("go")]}, config))
    reviewer_args = {"query": "dogs", "undeclared": True}
    result = agent.invoke(
        Command(
            resume={
                pauses["1"].id: {
                    "type": "edit",
                    "edited_action": {"name": "search", "args": reviewer_args},
                },
                pauses["2"].id: {
                    "type": "edit",
                    "edited_action": {"name": "delete_file", "args": {"path": 
"/b"}},
                },
            }
        ),
        config,
    )

    assert sorted(_RAN) == [
        ("delete_file", {"path": "/b", "call_id": "2"}),
        ("search", {"query": "dogs", "limit": 5}),
    ]
    messages = _tool_messages(result)
    assert messages["1"].content == (
        f"{_EDIT_NOTICE} Executed instead: search with arguments "
        '{"query": "dogs", "undeclared": true}.\n\nTool response:\nresults for 
dogs'
    )
    ai_message = next(m for m in result["messages"] if isinstance(m, AIMessage) 
and m.tool_calls)
    assert ai_message.tool_calls[0]["args"] == {"query": "cats"}


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("allowed", "decision", "loc", "message"),
    [
        (["approve", "edit"], {"type": "edit"}, ("edit", "edited_action"), 
"Field required"),
        (
            ["approve", "reject"],
            {"type": "edit", "edited_action": {"name": "search", "args": 
{"query": "x"}}},
            (),
            "expected tags: 'approve', 'reject'",
        ),
        (
            ["approve", "edit"],
            {"type": "edit", "edited_action": {"name": "delete_file", "args": 
{"path": "/"}}},
            ("edit", "edited_action", "name"),
            "Input should be 'search'",
        ),
        (
            ["approve", "edit"],
            {"type": "edit", "edited_action": {"name": "search", "args": 
{"limit": 1}}},
            ("edit", "edited_action", "args", "query"),
            "Field required",
        ),
    ],
)
@pytest.mark.parametrize("after", ["none", "retry", "error"])
def test_per_call_invalid_answer_is_not_kept(
    allowed: list[str], decision: dict[str, Any], loc: tuple[Any, ...], message:
str, after: str
) -> None:
    middleware = {
        "none": [],
        "retry": [ToolRetryMiddleware(max_retries=1, initial_delay=0, 
jitter=False)],
        "error": [ToolErrorMiddleware(lambda exc, _request: f"handled 
{exc!r}")],
    }[after]
    agent = _per_call_agent(
        [ToolCall(name="search", args={"query": "cats"}, id="1")],
        {"search": {"allowed_decisions": allowed}},
        middleware=middleware,
    )
    config: RunnableConfig = {"configurable": {"thread_id": "invalid"}}
    pause = _pauses(agent.invoke({"messages": [HumanMessage("go")]}, 
config))["1"]

    for _ in range(2):
        with pytest.raises(ValidationError) as exc_info:
            agent.invoke(Command(resume={pause.id: decision}), config)
        assert exc_info.value.error_count() == 1
        error = exc_info.value.errors()[0]
        assert error["loc"] == loc
        assert message in error["msg"]
        assert _RAN == []
        assert agent.get_state(config).interrupts == (pause,)

    result = agent.invoke(Command(resume={pause.id: {"type": "approve"}}), 
config)
    assert _RAN == [("search", {"query": "cats", "limit": 5})]
    assert _tool_messages(result)["1"].content == "results for cats"


async def test_per_call_invalid_answer_does_not_rerun_parallel_calls_async() -> 
None:
    started: list[str] = []

    @tool
    async def slow_search(query: str) -> str:
        """Search slowly."""
        started.append(query)
        await asyncio.sleep(0.1)
        return f"results for {query}"

    agent = _per_call_agent(
        [
            ToolCall(name="slow_search", args={"query": "cats"}, id="1"),
            ToolCall(name="search", args={"query": "dogs"}, id="2"),
        ],
        {"slow_search": True, "search": {"allowed_decisions": ["approve", 
"reject"]}},
        tools=[slow_search, search],
    )
    config: RunnableConfig = {"configurable": {"thread_id": "parallel"}}
    pauses = _pauses(await agent.ainvoke({"messages": [HumanMessage("go")]}, 
config))

    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        await agent.ainvoke(
            Command(resume={pauses["1"].id: {"type": "approve"}, pauses["2"].id:
{"type": "edit"}}),
            config,
        )
    assert started == ["cats"]
    assert _RAN == []

    result = await agent.ainvoke(Command(resume={pauses["2"].id: {"type": 
"approve"}}), config)
    assert started == ["cats"]
    assert _RAN == [("search", {"query": "dogs", "limit": 5})]
    messages = _tool_messages(result)
    assert (messages["1"].content, messages["2"].content) == ("results for 
cats", "results for dogs")


async def test_per_call_edit_async() -> None:
    agent = _per_call_agent([ToolCall(name="search", args={"query": "cats"}, 
id="1")], {"search": True})
    config: RunnableConfig = {"configurable": {"thread_id": "async-edit"}}
    pause = _pauses(await agent.ainvoke({"messages": [HumanMessage("go")]}, 
config))["1"]
    edit = {"type": "edit", "edited_action": {"name": "search", "args": 
{"query": "dogs", "limit": 2}}}
    result = await agent.ainvoke(Command(resume={pause.id: edit}), config)

    assert _RAN == [("search", {"query": "dogs", "limit": 2})]
    assert _tool_messages(result)["1"].content.endswith("Tool response:\nresults
for dogs")


def test_per_call_retried_tool_is_not_reviewed_again() -> None:
    attempts: list[str] = []

    @tool
    def flaky(query: str) -> str:
        """Fail on the first attempt."""
        attempts.append(query)
        if len(attempts) == 1:
            msg = "transient"
            raise RuntimeError(msg)
        return f"results for {query}"

    agent = _per_call_agent(
        [ToolCall(name="flaky", args={"query": "cats"}, id="1")],
        {"flaky": True},
        tools=[flaky],
        middleware=[ToolRetryMiddleware(max_retries=2, initial_delay=0, 
jitter=False)],
    )
    config: RunnableConfig = {"configurable": {"thread_id": "retry"}}
    pause = _pauses(agent.invoke({"messages": [HumanMessage("go")]}, 
config))["1"]
    result = agent.invoke(Command(resume={pause.id: {"type": "approve"}}), 
config)

    assert "__interrupt__" not in result
    assert attempts == ["cats", "cats"]
    assert _tool_messages(result)["1"].content == "results for cats"


@pytest.mark.parametrize("tool_call_id", [None, ""])
def test_per_call_tool_call_id(tool_call_id: str | None) -> None:
    agent = _per_call_agent(
        [ToolCall(name="search", args={"query": "cats"}, id=tool_call_id)], 
{"search": True}
    )
    config: RunnableConfig = {"configurable": {"thread_id": "ids"}}
    if tool_call_id is None:
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="Tool call `search` has no ID"):
            agent.invoke({"messages": [HumanMessage("go")]}, config)
        return
    pause = agent.invoke({"messages": [HumanMessage("go")]}, 
config)["__interrupt__"][0]
    assert pause.value["tool_call_id"] == ""
    agent.invoke(Command(resume={pause.id: {"type": "approve"}}), config)
    assert _RAN == [("search", {"query": "cats", "limit": 5})]

Action: FileEditorAction

Arguments:
  command: "create"
  path: "/tmp/scratch/per_call_section.py"
  file_text: 
    
    
    # --- per_call mode 
---------------------------------------------------------------
    
    _RAN: list[tuple[str, dict[str, Any]]] = []
    
    
    @tool
    def search(query: str, limit: int = 5) -> str:
        """Search the web."""
        _RAN.append(("search", {"query": query, "limit": limit}))
        return f"results for {query}"
    
    
    @tool
    def delete_file(path: str, runtime: ToolRuntime) -> str:
        """Delete a file."""
        _RAN.append(("delete_file", {"path": path, "call_id": 
runtime.tool_call_id}))
        return f"deleted {path}"
    
    
    @pytest.fixture
    def ran() -> list[tuple[str, dict[str, Any]]]:
        _RAN.clear()
        return _RAN
    
    
    def _per_call_agent(
        tool_calls: list[ToolCall],
        interrupt_on: dict[str, Any],
        *,
        tools: list[Any] | None = None,
        middleware: list[Any] | None = None,
        interrupt_mode: InterruptMode = "per_call",
    ) -> Any:
        return create_agent(
            model=FakeToolCallingModel(tool_calls=[tool_calls, []]),
            tools=tools or [search, delete_file],
            middleware=[
                HumanInTheLoopMiddleware(interrupt_on, 
interrupt_mode=interrupt_mode),
                *(middleware or []),
            ],
            checkpointer=InMemorySaver(),
        )
    
    
    def _pauses(result: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Interrupt]:
        return {i.value["tool_call_id"]: i for i in result["__interrupt__"]}
    
    
    def _tool_messages(result: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, ToolMessage]:
        return {m.tool_call_id: m for m in result["messages"] if isinstance(m, 
ToolMessage)}
    
    
    def test_interrupt_mode_defaults_to_batched_and_rejects_unknown_modes() -> 
None:
        assert get_args(InterruptMode) == ("batched", "per_call")
        assert HumanInTheLoopMiddleware({"search": True}).interrupt_mode == 
"batched"
        with pytest.raises(
            ValueError,
            match=re.escape("`interrupt_mode` must be 'batched' or 'per_call', 
got 'sometimes'."),
        ):
            HumanInTheLoopMiddleware({"search": True}, 
interrupt_mode="sometimes")  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    
    
    def test_per_call_pauses_each_listed_call_before_it_runs(
        ran: list[tuple[str, dict[str, Any]]],
    ) -> None:
        runtimes: list[Any] = []
    
        def describe(tool_call: ToolCall, state: AgentState[Any], runtime: 
Runtime[Any]) -> str:
            runtimes.append(runtime)
            return f"Delete {tool_call['args']['path']}? 
({len(state['messages'])} messages)"
    
        agent = _per_call_agent(
            [
                ToolCall(name="search", args={"query": "cats"}, id="1"),
                ToolCall(name="delete_file", args={"path": "/a"}, id="2"),
                ToolCall(name="search", args={"query": "skip"}, id="3"),
                ToolCall(name="delete_file", args={"path": "/b"}, id="4"),
            ],
            {
                "search": {
                    "allowed_decisions": ["approve", "reject"],
                    "description": "Search?",
                    "when": lambda request: request.tool_call["args"]["query"] 
!= "skip",
                },
                "delete_file": {"allowed_decisions": ["approve"], "description":
describe},
            },
        )
        config: RunnableConfig = {"configurable": {"thread_id": "pauses"}}
        result = agent.invoke({"messages": [HumanMessage("go")]}, config)
    
        assert ran == [("search", {"query": "skip", "limit": 5})]
        pauses = _pauses(result)
        assert [pauses[i].value for i in ("1", "2", "4")] == [
            {
                "type": "tool_approval",
                "tool_call_id": "1",
                "name": "search",
                "args": {"query": "cats"},
                "description": "Search?",
            },
            {
                "type": "tool_approval",
                "tool_call_id": "2",
                "name": "delete_file",
                "args": {"path": "/a"},
                "description": "Delete /a? (2 messages)",
            },
            {
                "type": "tool_approval",
                "tool_call_id": "4",
                "name": "delete_file",
                "args": {"path": "/b"},
                "description": "Delete /b? (2 messages)",
            },
        ]
        assert runtimes
        assert all(type(runtime) is Runtime for runtime in runtimes)
        assert (
            pauses["1"].response_schema
            == TypeAdapter(_decision_schema(["approve", "reject"], "search", 
search)).json_schema()
        )
    
        result = agent.invoke(
            Command(resume={pause.id: {"type": "approve"} for pause in 
pauses.values()}), config
        )
        assert "__interrupt__" not in result
        assert sorted(_tool_messages(result)) == ["1", "2", "3", "4"]
        assert sorted(ran, key=str) == sorted(
            [
                ("search", {"query": "skip", "limit": 5}),
                ("search", {"query": "cats", "limit": 5}),
                ("delete_file", {"path": "/a", "call_id": "2"}),
                ("delete_file", {"path": "/b", "call_id": "4"}),
            ],
            key=str,
        )
    
    
    def test_per_call_unlisted_tools_run_without_pausing(
        ran: list[tuple[str, dict[str, Any]]],
    ) -> None:
        agent = _per_call_agent(
            [ToolCall(name="search", args={"query": "cats"}, id="1")], 
{"delete_file": True}
        )
        result = agent.invoke(
            {"messages": [HumanMessage("go")]}, {"configurable": {"thread_id": 
"unlisted"}}
        )
        assert "__interrupt__" not in result
        assert ran == [("search", {"query": "cats", "limit": 5})]
    
    
    def test_decision_schema_structure() -> None:
        schema = TypeAdapter(
            _decision_schema(["reject", "edit", "approve", "respond"], "search",
search)
        ).json_schema()
    
        assert schema["oneOf"] == [
            {"$ref": "#/$defs/RejectDecision"},
            {"$ref": "#/$defs/EditDecision"},
            {"$ref": "#/$defs/ApproveDecision"},
            {"$ref": "#/$defs/RespondDecision"},
        ]
        defs = schema["$defs"]
        assert defs["EditDecision"]["properties"]["edited_action"] == {
            "$ref": "#/$defs/EditedAction"
        }
        edited_action = defs["EditedAction"]["properties"]
        assert edited_action["name"]["const"] == "search"
        assert edited_action["args"] == {"$ref": "#/$defs/search"}
        tool_schema = search.tool_call_schema.model_json_schema()  # type: 
ignore[union-attr]
        assert defs["search"]["description"] == "Search the web."
        assert defs["search"]["properties"] == tool_schema["properties"]
        assert defs["search"]["required"] == ["query"]
    
        single = TypeAdapter(_decision_schema(["approve"], "search", 
search)).json_schema()
        assert "oneOf" not in single
        assert single["properties"] == {
            "type": {"const": "approve", "title": "Type", "type": "string"}
        }
    
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="deny"):
            _decision_schema(["approve", "deny"], "search")  # type: 
ignore[list-item]
    
    
    def test_decision_schema_shows_but_does_not_enforce_unvalidated_args() -> 
None:
        json_schema_tool = StructuredTool(
            name="lookup",
            description="Look something up.",
            args_schema={
                "type": "object",
                "properties": {"key": {"type": "string"}},
                "required": ["key"],
            },
            func=lambda **kwargs: kwargs,
        )
        for tool_name, tool_ in [("lookup", json_schema_tool), ("ghost", None)]:
            adapter = TypeAdapter(_decision_schema(["edit"], tool_name, tool_))
            args_def = adapter.json_schema()["$defs"][tool_name]
            edit = {"type": "edit", "edited_action": {"name": tool_name, "args":
{"other": 1}}}
            assert adapter.validate_python(edit) == edit
            with pytest.raises(ValidationError) as exc_info:
                adapter.validate_python({**edit, "edited_action": {"name": 
tool_name, "args": 1}})
            assert [e["loc"] for e in exc_info.value.errors()] == 
[("edited_action", "args")]
            if tool_ is None:
                assert args_def == {"title": "ghost", "type": "object"}
            else:
                assert args_def["properties"] == {"key": {"type": "string"}}
                assert args_def["required"] == ["key"]
                assert args_def["description"] == "Look something up."
    
    
    def test_per_call_decisions_produce_batched_tool_messages(
        ran: list[tuple[str, dict[str, Any]]],
    ) -> None:
        tool_calls = [
            ToolCall(name="search", args={"query": "cats"}, id="1"),
            ToolCall(name="search", args={"query": "dogs"}, id="2"),
            ToolCall(name="search", args={"query": "owls"}, id="3"),
            ToolCall(name="search", args={"query": "bats"}, id="4"),
        ]
        decisions: list[dict[str, Any]] = [
            {"type": "approve"},
            {"type": "reject"},
            {"type": "reject", "message": "not now"},
            {"type": "respond", "message": "the human's answer"},
        ]
        batched = _per_call_agent(tool_calls, {"search": True}, 
interrupt_mode="batched")
        batched_config: RunnableConfig = {"configurable": {"thread_id": 
"batched"}}
        batched.invoke({"messages": [HumanMessage("go")]}, batched_config)
        expected = _tool_messages(
            batched.invoke(Command(resume={"decisions": decisions}), 
batched_config)
        )
        ran.clear()
    
        agent = _per_call_agent(tool_calls, {"search": True})
        config: RunnableConfig = {"configurable": {"thread_id": "per-call"}}
        pauses = _pauses(agent.invoke({"messages": [HumanMessage("go")]}, 
config))
        resume = {
            pauses[str(call["id"])].id: decision
            for call, decision in zip(tool_calls, decisions, strict=True)
        }
        result = agent.invoke(Command(resume=resume), config)
    
        assert ran == [("search", {"query": "cats", "limit": 5})]
        messages = _tool_messages(result)
        assert sorted(messages) == sorted(expected)
        for tool_call_id, message in expected.items():
            assert messages[tool_call_id].content == message.content
            assert messages[tool_call_id].status == message.status
    
    
    def test_per_call_edit_runs_reviewers_args(ran: list[tuple[str, dict[str, 
Any]]]) -> None:
        agent = _per_call_agent(
            [
                ToolCall(name="search", args={"query": "cats"}, id="1"),
                ToolCall(name="delete_file", args={"path": "/a"}, id="2"),
            ],
            {"search": True, "delete_file": True},
        )
        config: RunnableConfig = {"configurable": {"thread_id": "edit"}}
        pauses = _pauses(agent.invoke({"messages": [HumanMessage("go")]}, 
config))
        reviewer_args = {"query": "dogs", "undeclared": True}
        result = agent.invoke(
            Command(
                resume={
                    pauses["1"].id: {
                        "type": "edit",
                        "edited_action": {"name": "search", "args": 
reviewer_args},
                    },
                    pauses["2"].id: {
                        "type": "edit",
                        "edited_action": {"name": "delete_file", "args": 
{"path": "/b"}},
                    },
                }
            ),
            config,
        )
    
        assert sorted(ran, key=str) == [
            ("delete_file", {"path": "/b", "call_id": "2"}),
            ("search", {"query": "dogs", "limit": 5}),
        ]
        messages = _tool_messages(result)
        assert messages["1"].content == (
            f"{_EDIT_NOTICE} Executed instead: search with arguments "
            '{"query": "dogs", "undeclared": true}.\n\nTool response:\nresults 
for dogs'
        )
        ai_message = next(m for m in result["messages"] if isinstance(m, 
AIMessage) and m.tool_calls)
        assert ai_message.tool_calls[0]["args"] == {"query": "cats"}
    
    
    def test_per_call_edit_still_applies_tool_validators(
        ran: list[tuple[str, dict[str, Any]]],
    ) -> None:
        class Positive(BaseModel):
            n: int
    
            @field_validator("n")
            @classmethod
            def check_positive(cls, n: int) -> int:
                if n <= 0:
                    msg = "n must be positive"
                    raise ValueError(msg)
                return n
    
        @tool(args_schema=Positive)
        def count(n: int) -> str:
            """Count to n."""
            ran.append(("count", {"n": n}))
            return str(n)
    
        agent = _per_call_agent(
            [ToolCall(name="count", args={"n": 3}, id="1")], {"count": True}, 
tools=[count]
        )
        config: RunnableConfig = {"configurable": {"thread_id": "validators"}}
        pause = _pauses(agent.invoke({"messages": [HumanMessage("go")]}, 
config))["1"]
        edit = {"type": "edit", "edited_action": {"name": "count", "args": {"n":
-1}}}
        result = agent.invoke(Command(resume={pause.id: edit}), config)
    
        assert ran == []
        message = _tool_messages(result)["1"]
        assert message.status == "error"
        assert "n must be positive" in str(message.content)
    
    
    @pytest.mark.parametrize(
        ("allowed", "decision", "loc", "message"),
        [
            (["approve", "edit"], {"type": "edit"}, ("edit", "edited_action"), 
"Field required"),
            (
                ["approve", "reject"],
                {"type": "edit", "edited_action": {"name": "search", "args": 
{"query": "x"}}},
                (),
                "expected tags: 'approve', 'reject'",
            ),
            (
                ["approve", "edit"],
                {"type": "edit", "edited_action": {"name": "delete_file", 
"args": {"path": "/"}}},
                ("edit", "edited_action", "name"),
                "Input should be 'search'",
            ),
            (
                ["approve", "edit"],
                {"type": "edit", "edited_action": {"name": "search", "args": 
{"limit": 1}}},
                ("edit", "edited_action", "args", "query"),
                "Field required",
            ),
        ],
    )
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("after", ["none", "retry", "error"])
    def test_per_call_invalid_answer_is_not_kept(
        ran: list[tuple[str, dict[str, Any]]],
        allowed: list[str],
        decision: dict[str, Any],
        loc: tuple[Any, ...],
        message: str,
        after: str,
    ) -> None:
        middleware = {
            "none": [],
            "retry": [ToolRetryMiddleware(max_retries=1, initial_delay=0, 
jitter=False)],
            "error": [ToolErrorMiddleware(lambda exc, _request: f"handled 
{exc!r}")],
        }[after]
        agent = _per_call_agent(
            [ToolCall(name="search", args={"query": "cats"}, id="1")],
            {"search": {"allowed_decisions": allowed}},
            middleware=middleware,
        )
        config: RunnableConfig = {"configurable": {"thread_id": "invalid"}}
        pause = _pauses(agent.invoke({"messages": [HumanMessage("go")]}, 
config))["1"]
    
        for _ in range(2):
            with pytest.raises(ValidationError) as exc_info:
                agent.invoke(Command(resume={pause.id: decision}), config)
            assert exc_info.value.error_count() == 1
            error = exc_info.value.errors()[0]
            assert error["loc"] == loc
            assert message in error["msg"]
            assert ran == []
            assert agent.get_state(config).interrupts == (pause,)
    
        result = agent.invoke(Command(resume={pause.id: {"type": "approve"}}), 
config)
        assert ran == [("search", {"query": "cats", "limit": 5})]
        assert _tool_messages(result)["1"].content == "results for cats"
    
    
    async def test_per_call_invalid_answer_does_not_rerun_parallel_calls_async(
        ran: list[tuple[str, dict[str, Any]]],
    ) -> None:
        started: list[str] = []
    
        @tool
        async def slow_search(query: str) -> str:
            """Search slowly."""
            started.append(query)
            await asyncio.sleep(0.1)
            return f"results for {query}"
    
        agent = _per_call_agent(
            [
                ToolCall(name="slow_search", args={"query": "cats"}, id="1"),
                ToolCall(name="search", args={"query": "dogs"}, id="2"),
            ],
            {"slow_search": True, "search": {"allowed_decisions": ["approve", 
"reject"]}},
            tools=[slow_search, search],
        )
        config: RunnableConfig = {"configurable": {"thread_id": "parallel"}}
        pauses = _pauses(await agent.ainvoke({"messages": [HumanMessage("go")]},
config))
    
        with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
            await agent.ainvoke(
                Command(
                    resume={pauses["1"].id: {"type": "approve"}, pauses["2"].id:
{"type": "edit"}}
                ),
                config,
            )
        assert started == ["cats"]
        assert ran == []
    
        result = await agent.ainvoke(Command(resume={pauses["2"].id: {"type": 
"approve"}}), config)
        assert started == ["cats"]
        assert ran == [("search", {"query": "dogs", "limit": 5})]
        messages = _tool_messages(result)
        assert messages["1"].content == "results for cats"
        assert messages["2"].content == "results for dogs"
    
    
    async def test_per_call_edit_async(ran: list[tuple[str, dict[str, Any]]]) ->
None:
        agent = _per_call_agent(
            [ToolCall(name="search", args={"query": "cats"}, id="1")], 
{"search": True}
        )
        config: RunnableConfig = {"configurable": {"thread_id": "async-edit"}}
        pause = _pauses(await agent.ainvoke({"messages": [HumanMessage("go")]}, 
config))["1"]
        edit = {
            "type": "edit",
            "edited_action": {"name": "search", "args": {"query": "dogs", 
"limit": 2}},
        }
        result = await agent.ainvoke(Command(resume={pause.id: edit}), config)
    
        assert ran == [("search", {"query": "dogs", "limit": 2})]
        assert str(_tool_messages(result)["1"].content).endswith("Tool 
response:\nresults for dogs")
    
    
    def test_per_call_retried_tool_is_not_reviewed_again() -> None:
        attempts: list[str] = []
    
        @tool
        def flaky(query: str) -> str:
            """Fail on the first attempt."""
            attempts.append(query)
            if len(attempts) == 1:
                msg = "transient"
                raise RuntimeError(msg)
            return f"results for {query}"
    
        agent = _per_call_agent(
            [ToolCall(name="flaky", args={"query": "cats"}, id="1")],
            {"flaky": True},
            tools=[flaky],
            middleware=[ToolRetryMiddleware(max_retries=2, initial_delay=0, 
jitter=False)],
        )
        config: RunnableConfig = {"configurable": {"thread_id": "retry"}}
        pause = _pauses(agent.invoke({"messages": [HumanMessage("go")]}, 
config))["1"]
        result = agent.invoke(Command(resume={pause.id: {"type": "approve"}}), 
config)
    
        assert "__interrupt__" not in result
        assert attempts == ["cats", "cats"]
        assert _tool_messages(result)["1"].content == "results for cats"
    
    
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("tool_call_id", [None, ""])
    def test_per_call_tool_call_id(
        ran: list[tuple[str, dict[str, Any]]], tool_call_id: str | None
    ) -> None:
        agent = _per_call_agent(
            [ToolCall(name="search", args={"query": "cats"}, id=tool_call_id)], 
{"search": True}
        )
        config: RunnableConfig = {"configurable": {"thread_id": "ids"}}
        if tool_call_id is None:
            with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="Tool call `search` has no 
ID"):
                agent.invoke({"messages": [HumanMessage("go")]}, config)
            assert ran == []
            return
        pause = agent.invoke({"messages": [HumanMessage("go")]}, 
config)["__interrupt__"][0]
        assert pause.value["tool_call_id"] == ""
        agent.invoke(Command(resume={pause.id: {"type": "approve"}}), config)
        assert ran == [("search", {"query": "cats", "limit": 5})]

+294|def test_per_call_edit_still_applies_tool_validators(
+295|    ran: list[tuple[str, dict[str, Any]]],
+296|) -> None:
+297|    class Positive(BaseModel):
+298|        n: int
+299|
+300|        @field_validator("n")
+301|        @classmethod
+302|        def check_positive(cls, n: int) -> int:
+303|            if n <= 0:
+304|                msg = "n must be positive"
+305|                raise ValueError(msg)
+306|            return n
+307|
+308|    @tool(args_schema=Positive)
+309|    def count(n: int) -> str:
+310|        """Count to n."""
+311|        ran.append(("count", {"n": n}))
+312|        return str(n)
+313|
+314|    agent = _per_call_agent(
+315|        [ToolCall(name="count", args={"n": 3}, id="1")], {"count": True}, 
tools=[count]
+316|    )

+318|    pause = _pauses(agent.invoke({"messages": [HumanMessage("go")]}, 
config))["1"]
+319|    edit = {"type": "edit", "edited_action": {"name": "count", "args": 
{"n": -1}}}
+320|    result = agent.invoke(Command(resume={pause.id: edit}), config)
+321|
+322|    assert ran == []
+323|    message = _tool_messages(result)["1"]
+324|    assert message.status == "error"
+325|    assert "n must be positive" in str(message.content)

Reasoning:
Making progress on the test, and the edit case is the sticking point. My test 
passed delete_file a search-style args object, which would trip validation on 
the args as well as the name. The spec asks for exactly one error at the real 
problem, so I'm wondering whether the args mismatch should count as a separate 
error or just a consequence of the wrong name.

I'm weighing whether a wrong tool name should produce only the name error, since
args can't sensibly be checked against a tool that wasn't named. One option is 
to validate the name first and skip the args check when it fails, perhaps by 
making EditedAction a discriminated union keyed on name, so a mismatch yields a 
single error at the edited_action level.

I'm considering a way to check the name before the args: if the name is wrong, 
the args check would be skipped, so the error would stay at the name alone. I'm 
checking whether the validator can see the name's result, since a field that 
failed validation wouldn't be there to read.

I'm considering a check that only validates the args when the name is correct, 
using the validator's view of whether the name field passed. If it did not, the 
args would be skipped and the name error would stand alone. I want to confirm 
that `info.data` actually exposes the earlier field inside a TypedDict.

One real gap: when an edit names another tool, the args also fail against this 
tool's schema, so there are two errors. I'll check the args only after the name 
is accepted, starting with whether pydantic passes earlier-validated TypedDict 
fields to a wrap validator.

from typing_extensions import TypedDict
from pydantic import TypeAdapter, WrapValidator, BaseModel, ValidationError
class A(BaseModel):
    q: str
def keep(v, handler, info):
    print('data', info.data)
    if 'name' in info.data: handler(v)
    return v

WrapValidator(keep)]})

+def _keep_reviewer_args(
+    args: Any, handler: ValidatorFunctionWrapHandler, info: ValidationInfo
+) -> Any:
+    """Validate the reviewer's args, keeping them as given so the tool receives
them.
+
+    Args of an action naming another tool are left unchecked, as they are not 
this
+    tool's: the name is the error.
+    """
+    if "name" in info.data:
+        handler(args)
+    return args

+def _edited_args_type(tool_name: str, tool: BaseTool | None) -> Any:
+    """Type of the args a reviewer may give `tool`, named after the tool.
+
+    When the model sees the tool's arguments as a pydantic model, the args are
+    validated against it. Otherwise the arguments the model sees are shown, if 
there is
+    a tool, and any object is accepted.
+    """
+    schema = tool.tool_call_schema if tool is not None else None
+    sees_schema = not (isinstance(tool, Tool) and not tool.args_schema)
+    if sees_schema and isinstance(schema, type) and issubclass(schema, 
BaseModel):
+        return Annotated[schema, WrapValidator(_keep_reviewer_args)]
+
+    shown: dict[str, Any] = {"title": tool_name, "type": "object"}
+    if tool is not None:
+        shown.update(convert_to_openai_function(tool)["parameters"])
+        if tool.description:
+            shown["description"] = tool.description
+
+    def show(json_schema: dict[str, Any], _: type[BaseModel]) -> None:
+        json_schema.clear()
+        json_schema.update(shown)
+
+    any_object = create_model(
+        tool_name, __config__=ConfigDict(extra="allow", json_schema_extra=show)
+    )
+    return Annotated[any_object, WrapValidator(_keep_reviewer_args)]
+

+def _edit_decision_type(tool_name: str, tool: BaseTool | None) -> Any:
+    """`EditDecision` whose action must call `tool_name`, with args fit for 
`tool`."""
+    edited_action = TypedDict(  # type: ignore[operator]
+        "EditedAction",
+        {"name": Literal[tool_name], "args": _edited_args_type(tool_name,

+def _decision_schema(
+    allowed_decisions: Sequence[DecisionType],
+    tool_name: str,
+    tool: BaseTool | None = None,
+) -> Any:
+    """Type of the answers a reviewer may give for a call to `tool_name`.
+
+    Each allowed decision is one member of a union tagged by `type`, in
+    `allowed_decisions` order. A single allowed decision is its own type. An 
`edit`
+    must name `tool_name`, and its args must fit the tool's arguments as the 
model sees
+    them; the args it carries are the reviewer's own, not the validated ones.
+
+    Args:
+        allowed_decisions: The decisions the reviewer may make.
+        tool_name: Name of the tool being called.
+        tool: The tool, whose arguments an `edit` is validated against.
+
+    Returns:
+        A type pydantic's `TypeAdapter` accepts.
+
+    Raises:
+        ValueError: If `allowed_decisions` is empty or names an unknown 
decision.
+    """
+    allowed = list(dict.fromkeys(allowed_decisions))
+    if not allowed or any(d not in get_args(DecisionType) for d in allowed):
+        msg = (
+            f"`allowed_decisions` for tool '{tool_name}' must be a non-empty 
list drawn from "
+            f"{list(get_args(DecisionType))}, got {list(allowed_decisions)!r}."
+        )
+        raise ValueError(msg)
+    decision_types: dict[str, Any] = {
+        "approve": ApproveDecision,
+        "reject": RejectDecision,
+        "respond": RespondDecision,
+    }
+    if "edit" in allowed:
+        decision_types["edit"] = _edit_decision_type(tool_name, tool)
+    members = tuple(decision_types[d] for d in allowed)
+    if len(members) == 1:
+        return members[0]
+    return Annotated[Union[members], Field(discriminator="type")]  # noqa: 
UP007

+    def _review(self, request: ToolCallRequest) -> tuple[Action | None, 
ToolMessage | None]:
+        """Pause for a reviewer's decision on the call about to run, if it 
needs one.
+
+        Returns:
+            The reviewer's replacement for the call, if they edited it, and the
tool
+                message to return without running the call, if they rejected it
or
+                responded on behalf of the tool.
+
+        Raises:
+            ValueError: If the call has no ID.
+        """
+        tool_call = request.tool_call
+        config = self.interrupt_on.get(tool_call["name"])
+        if config is None:
+            return None, None
+        when = config.get("when")
+        if when is not None and not when(request):
+            return None, None
+        if tool_call.get("id") is None:
+            msg = (
+                f"Tool call `{tool_call['name']}` has no ID, so it cannot be 
reviewed "
+                "on its own and matched with its result."
+            )
+            raise ValueError(msg)
+
+        action_request, _ = self._create_action_and_config(
+            tool_call, config, request.state, get_runtime()
+        )
+        approval_request = ToolApprovalRequest(
+            type="tool_approval",
+            tool_call_id=cast("str", tool_call["id"]),
+            name=tool_call["name"],
+            args=tool_call["args"],
+            description=action_request["description"],
+        )
+        decision: Decision = interrupt(
+            approval_request,
+            response_schema=_decision_schema(
+                config["allowed_decisions"], tool_call["name"], request.tool
+            ),
+        )
+        _, tool_message = self._process_decision(decision, tool_call, config)
+        if decision["type"] == "edit":
+            return decision["edited_action"], tool_message
+        return None, tool_message

Result:
Returns:
        `TypeAdapter` for a schema where each tool name is a `Literal` with its
            description.

    Raises:
        AssertionError: If `tools` is empty.
    """
    if not tools:
        msg = "Invalid usage: tools must be non-empty"
        raise AssertionError(msg)

    # Create a Union of Annotated Literal types for each tool name with 
description
    # For instance: Union[Annotated[Literal["tool1"], Field(description="...")],
...]
    literals = [
        Annotated[Literal[tool.name], Field(description=tool.description)] for 
tool in tools
    ]
    selected_tool_type = Union[tuple(literals)]  # type: ignore[valid-type]  # 
noqa: UP007

    description = "Tools to use. Place the most relevant tools first."

    class ToolSelectionResponse(TypedDict):
        """Use to select relevant tools."""

        tools: Annotated[list[selected_tool_type], 
Field(description=description)]  # type: ignore[valid-type]

    return TypeAdapter(ToolSelectionResponse)

Summary: finish: {"message": "Per-call review now works in both checkouts. An 
invalid answer raises `pydantic.ValidationError` and nothing of it is kept, so 
the same pause can be answered again. I couldn't run ruff or mypy because they 
aren't installed and there's no network, so please run lint and type checks 
before merging.\n\n**LangGraph**\n- `interrupt(value, response_schema=...)` 
takes a JSON Schema `dict` or a type pydantic can validate. A dict is passed 
through unchanged and not enforced. A type has its JSON Schema surfaced, each 
answer is validated before it is kept, and `interrupt` returns the validated 
object. The check lives only here, so it applies the same way to the middleware 
and your own graphs.\n- `Interrupt` has a new `response_schema` field (default 
`None`). It is surfaced, stored in checkpoints, shown in `repr` and in 
debug/task events, and added to the SDK's `Interrupt` type. When no schema is 
passed, `repr` and debug output are exactly as before.\n- The bug you described:
an ID-addressed answer used to be stored before anything checked it, which is 
why the thread kept replaying it. It is now held separately and only stored once
accepted.\n- **A rejected answer is treated as \"the pause is still pending\".**
Inside the node, `interrupt` raises an internal `GraphInterrupt` subclass 
(`ResumeRejected`), and the top-level graph raises the `ValidationError` to you.
I chose this because a plain error broke the async path: with two pauses 
answered at once (one valid, one not), LangGraph cancelled the other call while 
its tool was running, then ran that tool again on the next resume. Now parallel 
calls finish once. As a bonus, ToolNode, `ToolErrorMiddleware`, 
`ToolRetryMiddleware` and retry policies can't swallow a bad answer in any 
order.\n\n**LangChain (`human_in_the_loop.py`)**\n- `interrupt_mode` defaults to
`\"batched\"`, whose behaviour is unchanged. `InterruptMode` is the alias, and 
any other value raises the exact `ValueError` you specified.\n- With 
`\"per_call\"`, each listed call pauses in `wrap_tool_call` with the 
`tool_approval` payload you specified. A description factory gets the graph 
`Runtime`, `when` gets the real request, and a `None` ID raises.\n- 
`_decision_schema(allowed_decisions, tool_name, tool=None)` builds the answer 
type with the `oneOf`/`$defs` layout you specified. Each bad answer gives 
exactly one error at the problem; when an edit names another tool, only the name
is reported, since that tool's args don't apply.\n- An edit runs the reviewer's 
own args unchanged, so undeclared args are ignored, the tool's validators and 
`ToolRuntime` still apply, and the edit notice is kept. Reject and respond 
produce the same tool messages as batched mode (a test compares them 
directly).\n- For tools whose args are a plain JSON Schema, for legacy 
single-input `Tool`s (the model sees `__arg1`), and when there's no tool object,
the args the model sees are shown and any object is accepted.\n\n**Behaviour to 
know about**\n- Code inside a node that wraps `interrupt` in `except 
ValidationError` won't catch a rejection; only the caller of `invoke`/`ainvoke` 
sees the error.\n- A plain `Command(resume=value)` (not keyed by interrupt ID) 
is still saved by LangGraph as command input before any node runs, as it was 
before. A rejected plain answer is never accepted, but `invoke(None)` would 
retry it; a new `Command(resume=...)` replaces it. Answers keyed by ID, as you 
use them, are never stored.\n- If a previously accepted answer no longer fits 
(say the schema changed in code), it and the answers after it in that node are 
dropped and asked for again.\n- List the review middleware before 
`ToolRetryMiddleware`; otherwise each retry would be reviewed 
again.\n\n**Tests:** I added 29 test cases to LangGraph's 
`tests/test_interruption.py` (run against the memory and sqlite checkpointers 
and every durability mode) and 25 to `test_human_in_the_loop.py` (sync, async, 
parallel pauses, and the retry/error middleware). Several test plugins weren't 
installed and couldn't be downloaded, so I ran the suites through a throwaway 
shim outside the repos. Snapshot assertions always passed under it, so snapshot 
tests weren't really checked. Before/after runs of the full LangGraph, prebuilt,
checkpoint, SDK and LangChain agents suites show the same failures before and 
after, all caused by the missing plugins/services, and all new tests 
pass.\n\nNeither directory is a git repo, so nothing was committed.\n\n**Files 
changed**\n- LangGraph: `types.py`, `errors.py`, `pregel/_algo.py`,

**LangGraph**
- `interrupt(value, response_schema=...)` takes a JSON Schema `dict` or a type 
pydantic can validate. A dict is passed through unchanged and not enforced. A 
type has its JSON Schema surfaced, each answer is validated before it is kept, 
and `interrupt` returns the validated object. The check lives only here, so it 
applies the same way to the middleware and your own graphs.
- `Interrupt` has a new `response_schema` field (default `None`). It is 
surfaced, stored in checkpoints, shown in `repr` and in debug/task events, and 
added to the SDK's `Interrupt` type. When no schema is passed, `repr` and debug 
output are exactly as before.
- The bug you described: an ID-addressed answer used to be stored before 
anything checked it, which is why the thread kept replaying it. It is now held 
separately and only stored once accepted.
- **A rejected answer is treated as "the pause is still pending".** Inside the 
node, `interrupt` raises an internal `GraphInterrupt` subclass 
(`ResumeRejected`), and the top-level graph raises the `ValidationError` to you.
I chose this because a plain error broke the async path: with two pauses 
answered at once (one valid, one not), LangGraph cancelled the other call while 
its tool was running, then ran that tool again on the next resume. Now parallel 
calls finish once. As a bonus, ToolNode, `ToolErrorMiddleware`, 
`ToolRetryMiddleware` and retry policies can't swallow a bad answer in any 
order.

**LangChain (`human_in_the_loop.py`)**
- `interrupt_mode` defaults to `"batched"`, whose behaviour is unchanged. 
`InterruptMode` is the alias, and any other value raises the exact `ValueError` 
you specified.
- With `"per_call"`, each listed call pauses in `wrap_tool_call` with the 
`tool_approval` payload you specified. A description factory gets the graph 
`Runtime`, `when` gets the real request, and a `None` ID raises.
- `_decision_schema(allowed_decisions, tool_name, tool=None)` builds the answer 
type with the `oneOf`/`$defs` layout you specified. Each bad answer gives 
exactly one error at the problem; when an edit names another tool, only the name
is reported, since that tool's args don't apply.
- An edit runs the reviewer's own args unchanged, so undeclared args are 
ignored, the tool's validators and `ToolRuntime` still apply, and the edit 
notice is kept. Reject and respond produce the same tool messages as batched 
mode (a test compares them directly).
- For tools whose args are a plain JSON Schema, for legacy single-input `Tool`s 
(the model sees `__arg1`), and when there's no tool object, the args the model 
sees are shown and any object is accepted.

**Behaviour to know about**
- Code inside a node that wraps `interrupt` in `except ValidationError` won't 
catch a rejection; only the caller of `invoke`/`ainvoke` sees the error.
- A plain `Command(resume=value)` (not keyed by interrupt ID) is still saved by 
LangGraph as command input before any node runs, as it was before. A rejected 
plain answer is never accepted, but `invoke(None)` would retry it; a new 
`Command(resume=...)` replaces it. Answers keyed by ID, as you use them, are 
never stored.
- If a previously accepted answer no longer fits (say the schema changed in 
code), it and the answers after it in that node are dropped and asked for again.
- List the review middleware before `ToolRetryMiddleware`; otherwise each retry 
would be reviewed again.

**Tests:** I added 29 test cases to LangGraph's `tests/test_interruption.py` 
(run against the memory and sqlite checkpointers and every durability mode) and 
25 to `test_human_in_the_loop.py` (sync, async, parallel pauses, and the 
retry/error middleware). Several test plugins weren't installed and couldn't be 
downloaded, so I ran the suites through a throwaway shim outside the repos. 
Snapshot assertions always passed under it, so snapshot tests weren't really 
checked. Before/after runs of the full LangGraph, prebuilt, checkpoint, SDK and 
LangChain agents suites show the same failures before and after, all caused by 
the missing plugins/services, and all new tests pass.

Neither directory is a git repo, so nothing was committed.

**Files changed**
- LangGraph: `types.py`, `errors.py`, `pregel/_algo.py`, `pregel/_runner.py`, 
`pregel/_loop.py`, `pregel/debug.py`, `_internal/_scratchpad.py`, 
`sdk-py/langgraph_sdk/schema.py`, `tests/test_interruption.py`
- LangChain: `agents/middleware/human_in_the_loop.py` and its unit test file
